# TouchLine handover

For the people working on TouchLine: what it is, what works today, what is being built, where everything lives and how it is hosted. AI agents should start from [CLAUDE.md](../CLAUDE.md); the way we work together is in [CONTRIBUTING.md](../CONTRIBUTING.md).

## 0. Where things stand (2026-10-28, for the next agent: Claude, Codex or a person)

The owner's Claude subscription ends on 2026-10-29; work may continue in Codex. Start here, then read CLAUDE.md (the invariants) and the rest of this file.

**Live (touchline.quest):** engine 7 from this release (ENGINE_VERSION '7'), on top of engine 6 (#84), polish batch 3 (#85) and everything before. Online: Supabase project `yvqpmyqomkwtudxmkeou` with all migrations applied (the three engine 7 ones, `20261028000100` to `000300`, pasted by the owner on 2026-10-28 and recorded); Edge Functions `touchline-e7` (the loader pinning `verifier-533df0bd10128be8.js`, the Daily rotation's bundle, redeployed 2026-10-28 17:56 UTC), `touchline-e6` (keep to 2026-11-05 at least), `touchline-e5` (to 2026-11-04), `touchline-e4` (to 2026-11-03), all Verify JWT off. The sign-in and sign-up emails send a six-digit code only (templates in docs/ONLINE.md "Email templates", set by the owner).

**Not yet merged, on GitHub, each based on `release-e7` (integrate them into one batch from the latest `main`, real merges, full checks, one PR):**
- `claude/r8-ui-front`, `claude/r8-ui-season`, `claude/r8-ui-phone`, `claude/r8-ui-modes`: the interface passes (Home de-cluttering: drop the tiles that repeat the top menu, fold the Daily strip into the Daily card).
- `claude/r8-anim`: season and match-day motion, ball and kick sounds synced to the pitch, the redesigned stadium sounds.
- `claude/r8-season-play`: Season and Dynasty week loop, market and Inbox usability.
- `claude/r8-fm-depth`: Football Manager depth (club negotiations with counters, transfer requests from unhappy players, promises, player talks, squad status); every roll on a forked copy so untouched games play as before.
- `claude/r8-qa`: QA round 3 on engine 7 (look for commits titled `SEVERE:` first).
- `claude/r8-copy`: the rest of the strings onto `t()`, one name per thing.
- `claude/r8-audit-engine`, `-audit-logic-a`, `-audit-logic-b`, `-audit-screens`, `-audit-ui`, `-audit-online`: file-by-file code audits. `PERF:` commits are byte-identical; `FIX (moves draws):` and `BUNDLED:` commits change outcomes or the edge bundle and belong to an engine 8 release (bump, re-baseline pins, new loader `touchline-e8`, a midnight UTC release so the Daily pins on the new engine).
- `claude/r8-onboarding`, `claude/r8-share`, `claude/r8-trophies`, `claude/r8-tests`, `claude/r8-explore`, `claude/r8-a11y`.
- Shipped: `claude/e7-daily` (from the shipped `batch4-e7` cd83e24; released in #87, loader redeployed): the Daily rotating through the seven leagues from 2026-10-30 as a dated epoch (`DAILY_EPOCHS`), and an easier Super League (the rule `superEase`, one rating point off its rivals). An engine 7 bundle update, no `ENGINE_VERSION` bump: it needs a `touchline-e7` loader redeploy and must ship **before 2026-10-31 00:00 UTC** (2026-10-30 is a Premier League day, identical on both builds); later, move the rotation's first day to a future day (docs/ONLINE.md, "Release the Daily rotation and the eased Super League").

**Release rules learned the hard way:** batch work and push once (CI on PRs only; pushes run only on `main`); record a squash with `git merge -s ours` only when the branch holds a commit with the squash's tree; an engine bump goes live just before 00:00 UTC (the site first, then the new loader) so the next Daily pins on it; never edit an applied migration; never touch another Supabase project or Render workspace (the owner has others, including Trade Desk; `public.telegram_market_snapshots` in this project is not TouchLine's: leave it).

**Waiting on the owner:** the iOS app (later, their call), the ",NN" names data rebuild, a licence, Google and Apple sign-in, leaked-password protection and the Supabase paid plan before a public launch.

## 1. What TouchLine is

A free web football game that fuses the wheel draft of **38-0** with the depth of **Football Manager**. A wheel lands on a real club in a real season, you draft one player from that squad, repeat until your side is built, then either simulate the season instantly or manage it week by week. Your side takes one real club's place in a real league season and plays the other clubs' real squads.

- Seven leagues: Premier League, La Liga, Serie A, Bundesliga, Ligue 1, Eredivisie, Primeira Liga, seasons 2008-09 to 2026-27 (69,155 player-seasons), plus the Premier League's classic seasons 1992-93 to 2007-08 (8,903 player-seasons with estimated ratings, opt-in).
- Runs entirely in the browser, installable and playable offline. Saves live in the browser (`localStorage`). No account needed; an optional account adds cloud saves, the world leaderboards and online head-to-head rooms.
- Deterministic: one seed always plays out the same way on every device, which is what makes the Daily, challenge links, the online leaderboards and the online rooms fair (the server replays every ranked run).

## 2. What it does today

Everything below is on `main` and live on https://touchline.quest, through PR #83 (squash `01548e4`, 2026-10-27), on engine 5. Engine 6 and the second polish wave go live just before 00:00 UTC on 2026-10-28 from `release-e6` (section 3); the lines below already describe a few of its fixes where they change nothing a player would notice.

**Modes**
- **Quick Draft** (the 38-0 mode): a one-page 38-0 setup (Easy, Normal or Hard; ratings on or off; Squad First or Position First; Season or Prime ratings, Prime by default; era presets and a season slider; every formation tile drawn as a mini pitch), draft an XI and a bench of up to five (one place by default), then Squad complete: the pundits' prediction (the chance of a perfect and an unbeaten season among it), a scouting report on the XI and the manager's edge. The manager is spun (three reels of real managers from the clubs drafted, pick one) or picked; the draft ends with a recap (a grade from A to D, the best pick, the one that got away, the Hindsight XI: the best XI the same spins held) and a case (a power-up for the Profile locker, unpacked in a later draft, or a rare Icon who can take a drafted starter's place, a non-standard season). Set the captain, penalty taker and instructions at kick-off, then watch the season game by game beside a live table, with three bench changes and injury cover, highlights of any game, and a January window that offers the January spin (a tiered case reel onto a wheel player), the January Upgrader (one roll on one of the XI), the transfer market or Skip. After the league come the Cup and the Continental Cup, each revealed round by round in a full bracket and ending on our own cartoon trophy lifted; the league with both cups is the Treble, with its own celebration. The 38-0 chase (win every game) runs through setup, playback, summary, profile and share text. Setup's Modes add Crazy, Hard, Underdog, Legends, Speedrun, Gamble and the Upgrader, and a power-up tray; spin odds show in every mode and always add up to 100% (marked rounded unless exact).
- **Super League** (a Quick Draft option): the XI plays 38 games against the 19 greatest club-seasons in the data (one season per club, each at its best by the engine's team strength), then the Continental Cup against the next fifteen; the field is the same for everyone.
- **Daily Challenge**: the same wheel for everyone each day (UTC), two re-spins, with streaks.
- **Daily Arena**: one arena puzzle a day for everyone (UTC), the Gauntlet and the Cup of 16 in turn on the Premier League wheel, with its own streak; one tap from Home's casual row.
- **Weekly Challenge**: one themed quick-draft wheel a week for everyone (ISO week, UTC), across all seven leagues, nine themes in turn (teammate chain, European Nights, early years, Moneyball, hard mode, one nation, modern game, park the bus, Realistic), its own streak.
- **Season**: draft 16 to 22 players, then manage every matchweek: friendlies, live matches, a cup, a January window, a board that can sack you. Sim to the next big match plays the routine weeks and stops before derbies, title deciders, cup semi-finals and finals, an open run-in, the January window and a board alarm.
- **Dynasty**: Season mode that never ends: ageing, contracts, retirements, youth intake, summer draft and summer window.
- **Arena: The Cup**: draft 16, then a knockout of 8, 16 or 32 real club-seasons in one sitting (or the one-off final against a giant by default): the wheel, the champions, the giants, a decade or this season's Europe; seeds kept apart in round one, a redraw each round, four days between ties with bans that carry for every side, and the AI ties played around yours, on to a winner once you are out. Each tie managed live through the two stops and a shoot-out with your own penalty order and keeper, "Sim this tie", or Sim to the end. Its own save slot, a bracket on the hub and the summary, a share text written tie by tie and a card, Best runs on the Profile page and the cabinet's arena cups by size.
- **Arena: The Gauntlet**: draft 14, then climb fifteen real club-seasons from the wheel, each from a stronger band than the last, away and at home in turn, until the first defeat; one lifeline (change a rung's draw), a bonus spin after rungs 2, 4, 6 and 8, managed live, by the assistant or Sim to the end. Everyone on a seed climbs the same ladder; rungs climbed go to Best runs and the cabinet's Gauntlet shelf.
- **Run-in**: take over a real club at matchweek 28 with its real squad, a table projected from the real final one and a brief (the title, the top four, survival), four spins to strengthen, then the last ten games with Sim to the next big match; a verdict against the real club, a share text, the Profile page's Run-ins table and the cabinet's run-in shelf. Its own save slot.
- **Head-to-head**: two managers on one device draft from the same spins and play the same season. **Online rooms** (online build): make a room, send the link (`#h=...`) or the code, and draft against a friend on their own device, guests welcome with no account; the server holds the seed, replays every pick and plays the season; online results go on their own Trophy Room shelf.

**Draft systems**: no dead spins, eight formations, era / one-club / one-nation filters, hard mode and blind mode (ratings hidden while choosing, shown once a player is in your squad), re-spins, wheel twists every third spin (Golden, Basement, Blind, Decade), the Moneyball wheel (a salary cap, every player priced by rating and position, never age), the Teammate Chain (every pick a former teammate of the one before), European Nights (draft from all seven leagues), classic seasons 1992-93 to 2007-08 (one Setup toggle, "Estimated ratings" labels everywhere), pick grades on a finished draft, ghost-draft challenge links (`#c=...`, short `#s=...` online) verified by replay, and the case reel with its synthesised sound (ticks timed from the strip's curve, landing sounds by rarity; the speaker toggle and Sound on the Profile page). Every rating says what it is: the overall on the badge, the number in a slot (the disc on the pitch) with its out-of-position cost, and the attribute figure in a tooltip.

**Management systems**: 34 roles and six team instructions, an xG match engine with live stops at half time and the 70th minute (talks, subs, touchline shouts), condition / sharpness / morale / familiarity, injuries and suspensions, training focus, squad status and playing time, player conversations, captain and set-piece takers, backroom staff, man-marking, press conferences and dressing-room events (a pending decision is one strip inline in the Dugout), the assistant's briefing, a domestic cup and an optional Continental Cup (Cup and Europe tabs on the same bracket), a transfer market with bids, loans and part-exchange, finances, board objectives, in-season development, awards, 35 achievements, record books against real seasons, a share card, save export / import.

**See your squad** (Season and Dynasty): the Dressing room tab (hierarchy, groups, atmosphere, happiness with a morale log), the Planner tab (depth and needs, contracts, the medical centre), personality labels, league awards on the summary, Inbox actions.

**Recruitment** (Season and Dynasty): league knowledge and attributes shown as ranges until a full scout (one a market day or a matchweek) reveals a player and his hidden traits; scouting assignments; a recruitment focus that narrows the market and brings two graded scout reports a matchweek; contract talks with any player (agent moods, locked and excluded terms, a signing fee, a release clause AI clubs can pay, appearance and goal bonuses); an agent who finds a buyer for a 5% cut; sell-on shares on bids; board requests (funds, a backroom budget, Dynasty youth facilities) and the wage-to-transfer slider on a three-column Club tab; a data hub on the Stats tab (xG, xA, goals against xG, per 90, an analyst's note every five matchweeks). Nothing moves a seeded result unless used.

**Match day**: live matches play on a Football Manager style dashboard (both XIs as numbered shirts with live ratings, a central 2D pitch, switchable widgets such as heat map, match events, xG story, pass map, live table and latest scores, a commentary banner and our XI along the bottom with condition and morale; tap a man in the strip and he is picked out on the pitch); instructions, mentality, set-piece takers, substitutions on a drag-and-drop board (five in three windows, half time free) and, from 70, a shout can change the match at any minute, and the last twenty minutes play live to the whistle; the Live tab replays a finished match on the same screen; phones get the pitch first and a tab strip. On a desktop the board fits the window with no page scroll. The pitch follows Football Manager's 2D view: phases of build-up (from the back, through midfield, the final third, counters, staged set pieces), the ball in flight over its shadow, the man on the ball's name, pass lines and xG-coloured shot lines, runs onto the ball, the camera on big chances and goal replays; 1x is a broadcast pace (0.5x to 4x, remembered per browser), and every goal holds the clock for a celebration. The Dugout remembers Watch, Auto-play or Sim result: Watch opens the half-time and 70th-minute decisions as a centred pop-up (Play on, No changes, Let the assistant decide, Finish the match, Full sim), Auto-play lets the assistant answer both stops, Sim result goes straight to the report. Full, Extended and Key-moments highlight levels, the assistant's line at 15, 30, 60 and 80, and P to pause.

**Lineups**: tap a pitch slot in the Dugout for a picker (a bottom sheet on a phone) of everyone who can play there, rated there, with the occupant and To bench on top; in the Draft a player with several open slots shows one chip per slot.

**Dugout at a glance** (display only): squad rows carry a form arrow, a last-five sparkline, a MOTM star and the morale face; the squad list switches between Overview, Form, Fitness and Contract views; a Next match tab holds the opposition report, our units against theirs and the team talk; Roles show each option's rating, a fit chip and familiarity; Fixtures open with a season calendar; the Table tab draws the league position by matchweek; the Inbox has filter chips and a pinned Needs you row; a window countdown, a deadline-day ticker and Done deals on the Market; Space plays the week, 1 to 0 open the tabs.

**Trophy Room**: the Profile page's cabinet counts every mode's titles, cups, 38-0s, invincibles and centurions, with their own shelves for the arena, the run-in and online head-to-head; twelve tiered badges (bronze, silver, gold) show progress; an honours board lists every Treble and Double season; a manager card downloads as a PNG, and Best seasons filters by mode. Older profiles keep their five trophy counts exactly.

**Online** (the live build carries the Supabase variables; every mode still works signed out and offline): create an account or sign in by email code from the top bar, a profile with a checked display name, a flag and a favourite club; cloud saves for the Quick Draft (with the Daily and both Weeklies), Season, Dynasty and head-to-head slots (arena runs and Run-ins too once `claude/r7-cloud-slots` ships with its migration); the World screen with the Daily board, the weekly challenge, all-time totals and best streaks, and the 38-0 chase (Quick Draft runs on server-issued wheels, five a day, posted from the Summary), each with a country filter; short challenge links; online head-to-head rooms; the Privacy and fair play page. Every ranked result is replayed by the Edge Function; a posted claim is only compared.

**Help on the page**: seven one-time coach marks (draft reveal, bench picks, Squad and Tactics tabs, half time, match report, market), each gone after Got it and brought back by Show tips again on the Profile page; a Key chip on the Squad panel labels the condition, morale and sharpness bars and adds a pitch legend; the manager spin and the case explain what each manager and item brings. Every modal is a real dialog (focus kept inside, Escape closes, focus returns).

**Feel and reach** (#70 to #83): the first ten minutes (Home's three steps and Play your first draft, a plain first spin, the first result's next steps); commentary with colour (goals by story, chances, cards, the occasion, the mood of a quiet minute), nineteen Trophy Room milestones, a pundit's verdict on every summary and varied inbox lines, all display only; a synthesised sound palette with a Match sounds switch; VoiceOver names, keyboard play, a skip link and text to 200 % ([ACCESSIBILITY.md](ACCESSIBILITY.md)); phones: lazy screens, the classic pack on demand, the save and the quick kick-off in workers, every asset precached for offline play, a first-download budget in CI ([PERFORMANCE.md](PERFORMANCE.md)); the notch and home-indicator safe areas; a screen error boundary, a Not saving pill when the storage is full, Retry for a league that could not load, and the save export through the share sheet where a download is refused ([QA.md](QA.md)).

**Look**: light and dark themes (the sun and moon in the top bar flips them and remembers the choice; Auto on the Profile page follows the device live); a night-navy palette with cyan and violet-magenta neon accents (Daylight is a cool lavender); one typeface, Sora; Home opens on our own cartoon drawing of a floodlit ground at night, a drawn picture on each mode card that never crops its subject, with Lucide icons in place of emoji. No photos. Shell screens fit one desktop window with boxes that scroll inside themselves, the top bar folds away, and a Boxes button on most screens ticks boxes on or off.

Full detail with formulas: [GAME_DESIGN.md](GAME_DESIGN.md). Data method: [DATA.md](DATA.md). Design research: [RESEARCH.md](RESEARCH.md). The online layer: [ONLINE.md](ONLINE.md).

- Guests are allowed in online rooms, so `touchline-e4` (and `touchline-e5`) run with Verify JWT off.
- Online head-to-head wins go on their own Trophy Room shelf (`Cabinet.h2hOnline`), never into the hot-seat or league totals.
- Engine 5 is released tonight, just after 00:00 UTC on 2026-10-27, accepting the W44 Weekly pause.
- The draft grade goes in the share text, without the Hindsight XI's gap.
- Only the Draft's left box keeps "On to kick-off" (the recap's copy goes).
- Home's waiting games stay mode first ("Quick Draft · Touchline XI").
- Dugout decisions stay inline (the strip), not a dialog.
- The playtest report of 2026-10-26 (`issues-2026-10-26.pdf`, page 1) is triaged: TL-07 (a knockout called a friendly) fixed in #63; TL-02 (a power-up window outliving its draft turn), TL-03 (rating labels), TL-06 (dialog semantics) and TL-08 (rounded odds) in #69; TL-01 (keeper substitutions), TL-04 (assistant placement), TL-05 (fixture venues) and INV-01 (development resets) in #65.

**2026-10-27**: the owner made the repository public (no licence chosen yet: all rights reserved, README "Licence").

## 4. Where things live

| Path | What |
| --- | --- |
| `src/engine/` | The deterministic game engine: RNG, draft and wheel, ratings, match engine, season, cups, market, events, the arena's knockouts. Pure TypeScript. |
| `src/game/` | The save format (`types.ts`), the Zustand store (`store.ts`) and the game rules on top of the engine (`logic/`). |
| `src/ui/` | React screens and components, one global stylesheet; `src/ui/online/` the online screens (Account, World, rooms). |
| `src/online/` | The optional online layer: client, sync, outbox, boards, rooms, and the verifier and Edge Function request flow (`server/`). Loaded only when the build carries `VITE_SUPABASE_*`. |
| `supabase/` | `migrations/` (every database change), `tests/sql/` (the SQL suites), `functions/touchline/` (the Edge Function's Deno adapter), `config.toml`. |
| `public/data/` | Generated league packs (committed). |
| `public/flags/` | Drawn SVG flags (flag-icons, MIT). |
| `scripts/import/` | The offline pipeline that builds the packs from public datasets. |
| `scripts/online/` | Verifier bundle, function staging and loader, smoke and SQL test scripts. |
| `public/sw.js`, `public/manifest.webmanifest`, `public/icons/` | Offline support and app install. |
| `ios/` | The Capacitor iOS project (coming on `ios-native`, not on `main` yet). |
| `docs/` | Design, data and research docs, this file; `docs/images/` the README's screenshots. |
| [docs/FEATURES.md](FEATURES.md) | The full feature list (the README keeps a short summary). |
| [docs/ONLINE.md](ONLINE.md) | The online layer: database, Edge Function, rooms, the 38-0 chase and the runbooks. |
| [docs/BACKLOG.md](BACKLOG.md) | The continuous-improvement backlog: rounds done, owner decisions, what is next. |
| [docs/QA.md](QA.md) | The pre-iOS QA hunt: what was tested, the 14 fixes and the open items. |
| [docs/PERFORMANCE.md](PERFORMANCE.md) | Phone performance: measurements, budgets, how to rerun them. |
| [docs/ACCESSIBILITY.md](ACCESSIBILITY.md) | What is supported for VoiceOver, keyboard and larger text, and what is left. |
| [docs/ENGINE6.md](ENGINE6.md) | The gameplay sweep's engine to-do list that engine 6 worked through. |
| `docs/IOS_RELEASE.md` | The iOS build and App Store steps (coming on `ios-native`). |
| `.github/workflows/` | `ci.yml` (typecheck, build, the first-download budget, dist-key check, verifier build and check on every PR and every push to `main`; the test suite on the PR only, in three shards beside the build), `db-tests.yml` (SQL suite on SQL changes), `keepalive.yml`, `supabase-deploy.yml` (manual). |

## 5. Run it

Needs Node 22.13 or later.

```bash
git clone https://github.com/Minhhh13/Touchline.git   # public since 2026-10-27; pushing needs collaborator access
cd Touchline
npm ci
npm run dev          # http://localhost:5173
npm test             # all Vitest suites (npm run test:fast while you work: the long sweeps left out)
npm run typecheck
npm run build        # production build into dist/
npx vite preview --port 4173 --strictPort   # serve the build
```

Rebuilding the player data is rarely needed and downloads several large archives the first time: `node scripts/import/build.mjs`, with `DATA_CACHE=/path/to/datasets` to reuse a cache (default `.cache/datasets`; see [DATA.md](DATA.md)).

## 6. Hosting and backend

### Render (hosting)

Both sites are static sites in their own Render workspace **TouchLine** (workspace id `tea-dbeoghbrjlhs738bpcng`). Nothing else lives there. The owner's other projects are in a different Render workspace; never touch them.

| Service | URL | Tracks | Deploys | Use |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `Touchline` (`srv-dbeoi38m7kps73ei85dg`) | **https://touchline.quest** (also https://touchline-16xv.onrender.com) | `main` | after CI passes on the commit | The live game |
| `touchline-newfeature-preview` (`srv-dbesgvrrjlhs738om2og`) | https://touchline-newfeature-preview.onrender.com | `claude/touchline-preview` | on every push | The testing site: finished features land here first, then go live |

- Build command `npm ci && npm run build`, publish directory `dist`, no `BASE_PATH`, `NODE_VERSION=22`.
- Render's GitHub app is installed on the repo, so both sites deploy on their own. A merge into `main` goes live a few minutes after CI turns green; a red CI run never deploys. A collaborator does not need Render access to ship: merging to `main` deploys by itself. Render access is only for logs and environment variables, and then only to the TouchLine workspace, never the other one.
- Environment variables: the live site carries `VITE_SUPABASE_URL` and `VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY` (the project URL and its publishable key, both public), so the online layer is live. Vite bakes them into the build, so changing them needs a redeploy. The preview site is meant to stay offline-only ([ONLINE.md](ONLINE.md) section 8, item 7).
- Custom domain: `touchline.quest`, registered at Porkbun (DNS: an A record `216.24.57.1` for the bare domain, a CNAME `www` to `touchline-16xv.onrender.com`, plus Resend's four email records). Render issues the HTTPS certificates. The owner holds the Porkbun login; edit only the touchline.quest DNS, never the owner's other domains.
- Headers for the live site (security headers, a report-only CSP naming the Supabase project, cache rules for `sw.js`, `build.json`, `assets` and `data`): the table in [ONLINE.md](ONLINE.md) section 8, set in the Render dashboard.
- There is no GitHub Pages workflow. It failed on every push to `main` while the repo was private, and because Render waits for every check to pass, it blocked the live deploy; do not add one back now the repo is public (Render hosts).

### Supabase (backend for the online layer)

- Organisation **Team TouchLine**, project **TouchLine**, id `yvqpmyqomkwtudxmkeou`, region Sydney (ap-southeast-2), Postgres 17, Free plan. The owner keeps this project and upgrades it to a paid plan before a public launch. Never touch any other Supabase project. The friend's Supabase access is to the Team TouchLine organisation only.
- Schema, all applied:
  - Phase 1, the ten migrations in `supabase/migrations/` from `20261025092919_online_core.sql` to `20261025131500_storage_size_accounting.sql` (profiles, cloud saves, the Daily and weekly boards with all-time totals, challenge codes, housekeeping, deny policies, the two review hardenings, the keep-alive, Storage-size accounting), recorded under the file versions; then `20261025183312_display_name_check.sql`.
  - The 38-0 chase, `20261026090000_chase_board.sql` (`private.chase_attempts`, `private.chase_entries`, `svc_chase_*`, the readers `chase_board`, `chase_summary`, `chase_my_status`, `chase_my_entries`): applied by the owner in the SQL editor on 2026-10-26, **not recorded in the migration history** (the file re-runs safely).
  - Online rooms, `20261026120000_h2h_rooms.sql`: `private.h2h_rooms` with RLS deny-all, the reader `h2h_peek` for `anon`, the writers `svc_h2h_room_*` for `service_role` only, the pg_cron job `touchline-h2h-rooms` every 10 minutes. Applied by the owner in the SQL editor on 2026-10-26, **not recorded in the history either** (section 7).
  - Not applied yet: error reports, `20261028000100_error_reports.sql` (`private.error_reports` with RLS deny-all, the writer `svc_error_report` for `service_role` only, the owner's view `private.error_report_summary`, the pg_cron job `touchline-error-reports` pruning at 30 days; branch `claude/r7-error-reporting`, runbook in [ONLINE.md](ONLINE.md) section 7).
  - Not applied yet: the casual boards, `20261028000200_casual_boards.sql` (`private.casual_puzzles`, `private.casual_entries`, `svc_casual_*`, the readers `casual_board`, `casual_summary`, `casual_my_entries`); the owner pastes it with the other two before the engine 7 release ([ONLINE.md](ONLINE.md) section 7, "Release engine 7").
  - Not applied yet: arena and Run-in cloud slots, `20261028000300_cloud_slots_arena_runin.sql` (the `cloud_saves.slot` check, the bucket's upload policy, `save_claim` and `save_delete` widened to `arena` and `runin`; no new function); with the other two in "Release engine 7". The three branches each named their file `20261027120000_*`; the batch 4 integration renumbered them so each version is unique.
  - The project's default privileges grant every new public table and function to `anon` and `authenticated`, so every migration revokes them first (tested by `supabase/tests/sql/phase1_security.sql`).
- Security advisor: only the pre-existing warning "leaked password protection disabled" (to switch on, section 8).
- Auth: accounts only (anonymous sign-ins off), Confirm email on, custom SMTP through Resend (domain `touchline.quest`). Site URL `https://touchline.quest`; redirect URLs in [ONLINE.md](ONLINE.md) section 3. Google sign-in still needs its OAuth client.
- Edge Function `touchline-e4`: deployed in its loader form, version 5, with **Verify JWT off** (owner-approved 2026-10-26, so guests can play rooms). Auth is `withSupabase({ auth: ['user', 'publishable'] })`: a guest with only the publishable key reaches the `room.*` ops and gets 401 on everything else (checked live: `daily.pin` answers "Sign in first"). It pins `edge/verifier-37d256f009b3b3e9.js` (SHA-256 `37d256f0…9b82`), which the live site serves; any change to that bundle needs a new loader deploy after the site deploys. Function secret `TOUCHLINE_SITE_URL` (default `https://touchline.quest`): its `build.json` and packs decide pins and the pack archive (private bucket `packs`). Engine 5 adds `touchline-e5` and keeps `touchline-e4` 7 days (section 3).
- Engine 5 (PR #65, merged at 23:42 UTC on 2026-10-26): its function is `touchline-e5` (`supabase/config.toml` `[functions.touchline-e5]`, Verify JWT off); `touchline-e4` stays deployed at least 7 days after it ships. Its loader keeps working because the e5 site also serves `edge/verifier-37d256f009b3b3e9.js` byte for byte from `public/edge/` (ONLINE.md section 7). No migration: the engine columns are free text.
- Engine 6 (`engine-e6`, releasing just before 00:00 UTC 2026-10-28): the next function is `touchline-e6` (`supabase/config.toml` `[functions.touchline-e6]`, Verify JWT off); `touchline-e5` stays deployed to at least 2026-11-04 and `touchline-e4` to 2026-11-03. Their loaders keep working because the e6 site serves both bundles byte for byte from `public/edge/` (`verifier-603b905d0570f641.js` for e5, built from `ec520f3`; `legacyEdge.test.ts` pins both). The site and the function go live before midnight so 2026-10-28's Daily is pinned on engine 6 (ONLINE.md section 7). No migration.
- Keep-alive: the pg_cron job `touchline-keepalive` runs inside the project at 05:23 UTC (a query on `daily_puzzles`, plus one GET of the project's own Data API through pg_net once the Vault secrets `touchline_project_url` and `touchline_publishable_key` exist; SQL in [ONLINE.md](ONLINE.md) section 3). As a second ping, `.github/workflows/keepalive.yml` reads one row of `daily_puzzles` every day at 04:41 UTC once the repository variables `SUPABASE_URL` and `SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY` are set. It never fails, so it cannot block Render.
- A build without `VITE_SUPABASE_*` is still the offline game exactly.
- Keys: the publishable (anon) key may go in the browser. The `service_role` key must never appear in the repo, in a `VITE_` variable or in browser code; it belongs only in Supabase secrets for Edge Functions.

## 7. Known issues and limits

Brought up to date on 2026-10-27 (`claude/r7-docs`): items that shipped or that engine 6 fixes are gone from this list and are recorded in the change log and [ENGINE6.md](ENGINE6.md). Add new items at the end of the group they belong to.

**Releases and the online layer**
- **Engine 6's release (just before 00:00 UTC on 2026-10-28).** The e6 site and `touchline-e6` must be live before midnight so 2026-10-28's Daily is pinned on engine 6: a Daily is pinned by the first ranked request of the UTC day, and a signed-in visitor on the old site before Render serves the new `build.json` (CI plus the deploy, about ten minutes, plus up to five minutes of the function's cached copy, `LIVE_BUILD_TTL_MS`) pins it on the old engine, so no new-engine run ranks that day ("Unranked today"). Engine 5 was merged at 23:42 UTC on 2026-10-26 for the same reason. Runbook: [ONLINE.md](ONLINE.md) section 7.
- **Weekly ranking pauses across an engine change.** A week's Weekly is pinned once, on the engine of its first ranked request, and a function verifies only its own engine's pins: W44 was pinned on engine 4, so no W44 Weekly ranks after engine 5 until Monday 2 November (the casual Weekly still plays). Engine 6 (Wednesday 2026-10-28) falls inside the same paused week, so W45, from Monday 2 November, is the first Weekly pinned on engine 6. Best practice: release an engine bump just after 00:00 UTC on a Monday.
- **Old engines' bundles are static files until their functions retire.** `public/edge/verifier-37d256f009b3b3e9.js` (engine 4, from `main` c897f2c) until `touchline-e4` retires on or after 2026-11-03, `public/edge/verifier-603b905d0570f641.js` (engine 5, from `engine-e5` at ec520f3, SHA-256 `603b905d…524d`) until `touchline-e5` retires on or after 2026-11-04, and from engine 7 (`batch4-e7`) `public/edge/verifier-f088fc41409257f9.js` (engine 6, from 45e3093, PR #84, SHA-256 `f088fc41…591d`; check each against the live site before the release, ONLINE.md section 7, "Release engine 7" step 1) until `touchline-e6` retires 7 days after engine 7 ships. `src/online/__tests__/legacyEdge.test.ts` pins all three; delete each file with its test entry and its `[functions.touchline-eN]` block when that function goes.
- **`room.pick` checks the room's engine from engine 6** (`roomMove` in `src/online/server/service.ts`: 409 `engine_mismatch` with `roomEngine`, as `room.join` does). `touchline-e5` and `touchline-e4` lack the check until they retire; the game never sends them another engine's room.
- **Two migrations are applied but not in the migration history.** `chase_board` and `h2h_rooms` were pasted into the SQL editor on 2026-10-26, so `list_migrations` does not show them and `supabase db push` would run them again (harmless: both re-run safely). To record them: `insert into supabase_migrations.schema_migrations (version, name) values ('20261026090000', 'chase_board'), ('20261026120000', 'h2h_rooms');`.
- **The Supabase connector's `apply_migration` approval never reaches the owner.** The working route for a migration is the owner pasting the file into the SQL editor (then recording the version as above, and running both advisors).
- **Free Supabase projects pause** after about a week idle; the keep-alive covers it once its Vault secrets or repository variables are set, and the plan is to upgrade before a public launch.
- Google sign-in needs the owner's Google Cloud OAuth client ([ONLINE.md](ONLINE.md) section 5c item 2); until it is set up the Account screen offers email only (the button appears by itself once the project reports Google on, or with `VITE_GOOGLE_AUTH=1`).
- The account smoke runs against mocks (Playwright routes for Auth, the Data API, Storage and the function): sending a real code would create an account on the project. The two-device sync on the real project and `scripts/online/smoke-function.mjs` need the owner's two test accounts; the two-device story is covered by `sync.test.ts` on the fake backend.
- Ranked boards: the Daily and weekly seeds are public and the season is deterministic, so a player can try many drafts offline and post only the best one. The verifier stops forged figures and extra re-spins, not offline rehearsal; it is the same for everyone and is how fixed-seed boards work (the 38-0 chase uses server-issued wheels for this reason). Look-alike names from other scripts (a Cyrillic "а" for a Latin "a") still pass the name rules; only invisible and spacing characters are refused.
- Saves are per browser; clearing site data loses them unless exported from the Profile page or, signed in, kept in the cloud (arena runs and Run-ins too once the `cloud_slots_arena_runin` migration is applied; until then they stay on the device). Since `polish-qaopen` the game asks the browser to keep its storage after the first finished result (O-9); the iOS wrapper's native mirror of the save is still to come.
- **Two tabs: the last page to save wins** (`claude/polish-saves`): the other pages stop saving and ask to be reloaded ("Saved in another tab"); anything done in a stale page since the other saved is lost on that reload, by design. Headless Chromium never used the back-forward cache in the smokes, so the `pageshow` check is proven by a unit test and a dispatched event only.
- **Guest rate limits key on the first `x-forwarded-for` address** (r8 audit online, 2026-10-28, open): `createEdgeHandler` in `src/online/server/edge.ts` hashes the first entry of `x-forwarded-for` into the guest's rate-limit subject (`guestSubject`). If the platform appends to a client-sent header rather than overwriting it, a guest can send a new address with every call and so a new subject, which gets past the hourly room and error-report limits and can fill the 300-room cap (`rooms_busy`) for everyone. The error reports' hourly cap of 2000 rows still holds. Check it on the live function before changing anything: two guest `room.create` calls carrying different `x-forwarded-for` headers should count against one subject. If they do not, key guests on a header the platform sets (Cloudflare's `cf-connecting-ip`, if the function receives it), as a `BUNDLED:` change with a loader redeploy.
- **The server's per-isolate caches** (r8 audit online, open, efficiency only): every `room.pick` reads the data-pack registry row again (`roomUniverse`, `dataFor` in `service.ts`), although a registered version never changes. The universe cache holds two universes keyed by league set, so three live rooms on three different league sets rebuild a universe from the pack archive on most picks. Both are worth a `BUNDLED:` change after the release (a memo of registry rows, and a larger cache or one universe per data version with every league).
- **A casual post on data newer than its pin loads that data's leagues** (r8 audit online, open, low): `submitCasual` asks `casualLeagues(board, seed, reg.leagues)` of the run's data, not the pin's settings as the Daily does (`puzzleLeagues(pin.settings)`), so a mid-week rebuild that appended a league could plan the themed Weekly's universe on other leagues and refuse an honest run. Only matters for a rebuild that adds a league mid-week.

**Data and rights**
- **Data licensing.** Ratings come from community Kaggle datasets derived from EA Sports FC ratings (FC 24 CC0, FC 25 Apache 2.0, FC 26 CC0, FC 27 an unofficial non-commercial snapshot), the European Soccer Database (ODbL), openfootball results (public domain) and Wikidata (CC0). The ratings themselves originate with EA. Treat TouchLine as a non-commercial fan project; going commercial would need a different ratings source. Full credits in [DATA.md](DATA.md).
- **No licence for the code yet.** The repository is public (2026-10-27) but all rights reserved until the owner chooses a licence; do not add one without the owner.
- **No real crests, kits, logos or player photos.** Clubs are shown as colours plus a letter code. Keep it that way. The pictures are our own drawings, with no photos ([CREDITS.md](CREDITS.md)).
- **Player names with a stray ",NN".** 54 names in the packs (53 players; on 89 rows) carry a suffix from the European Soccer Database ("D. Cousin,20", "P. Kenny,30"; 38 of them nation UNK). `tidyUniverseNames` cleans them for display at load; the packs keep them until a data rebuild strips them and re-matches the nation (BACKLOG round 3 #1: a new stamp, `packHashes.json`, pins re-baselined, deployed after 00:00 UTC). [DATA.md](DATA.md), "Known data faults".
- Nationality is unknown (`UNK`) on 1,889 rows (877 pack-and-player pairs) after the Wikidata override ([DATA.md](DATA.md)).
- Classic Premier League seasons can be chosen in Setup and in hot-seat head-to-head and are labelled, but not in online head-to-head rooms (the server's universe never loads the classic pack); Ligue 1, the Eredivisie and the Primeira Liga have no real tables (no record books there).
- Values and wages are our own model, not real figures.
- The setup screens open with an invented club name ("Ashvale United", "AFC Lowfield"), or the name the viewer last typed and played with (`touchline-team-name`); "Touchline XI" is now only the game logic's and the verifier's fallback (pinned by logic tests). A team name never enters a ranked settings hash (`puzzleOf` drops it), so the Daily and the Weekly default to an invented name as well.
- The default team name is still "Touchline XI" (set in the game logic and pinned by tests).
- **A data rebuild depends on the machine's time zone** (r8 audit online, 2026-10-28, open): `scripts/import/build.mjs` (`ageAt`, the snapshot window) and `historic/index.mjs` read the European Soccer Database's `"YYYY-MM-DD 00:00:00"` dates with `new Date()`, which takes local time. So the same inputs give other ages and rows on a UTC+ machine than in UTC, and two people's rebuilds can differ byte for byte. Rebuild with `TZ=UTC` until the script sets it itself. Also seen: queries and the final row sort have no full tie-breaker; cached openfootball files and Wikimedia "missing" answers are never fetched again; a failed `nations-override.json` read is swallowed. Each fix changes the packs, so it waits for a rebuild.

**Game rules waiting for an owner's call**
- **Contracts in a Dynasty still renew themselves, and a man who left can come back younger** ([ENGINE6.md](ENGINE6.md) E6-8): both change what the manager sees each summer (a renewal list or an Inbox item, and a bar on younger seasons of a man who left). Engine 6 did not take them.
- **Development under 30 never declines in-season.** Since engine 5 (INV-01) development is a delta from the shown overall. Unused men under 30 hold their rating in-season (growth needs minutes; decline starts at 30 and in the summer). An unused-player decline would be a new rule and a further bump.
- **Engine 6's Trebles are rarer.** With lower-scoring matches the drafted side wins fewer one-off ties: three seeds in 400 won the league and both cups (`cupBracket.test.ts` uses `treble-115`). The Gauntlet's Calibrated climb fell from 5.13 to 4.44 rungs (22.3 % to 11.3 % reaching ten) and Realistic rose from 1.73 to 1.93, both inside their guards.
- Recruitment choices for the owner to confirm: your own league's knowledge starts at 70, not the plan's 100, because a one-league game (the default) draws its whole market from other seasons of that league and would have nothing to scout; the league season you play in is always known exactly instead. A sell-on is read as a share offered to the selling club on a bid. A wage agreed in talks holds for the season; Dynasty ageing re-prices every wage in the summer as before. Contract bonuses and the clauses carry into the next Dynasty season; an agent stops shopping a player in the summer. Recruitment's masking covers the Market tab, approaches, the shortlist and the scout reports; Explore still shows every number, by design.
- Quick Draft bench: the match engine's injury rate means an injured starter is covered in roughly 2 to 17 of 38 games (measured on engine 5); injury cover only uses bench players who can play the slot, so a thin bench often leaves an injured man playing on.
- **Gauntlet: an era's ladder repeated a club** (fixed on engine 7, `claude/r7-engine7`): a club appeared once among the fifteen mains only when the pool had enough clubs per band; an era's smaller pool (2020-21 onwards: 140 club-seasons) fell back to an unused club-season of the band, so every such ladder had some club twice. `buildLadder` (`src/engine/gauntlet.ts`) now takes the nearest bands' new clubs first (`nearestFresh`), so all fifteen differ wherever the pool holds fifteen clubs (`gauntlet.test.ts`, three eras over 200 seeds each).
- **Run-in: the Bundesliga's relegation play-off** is played from engine 7 (`claude/r7-engine7`): a German run-in that finishes 16th plays two legs against the weakest club promoted for the next season (`playOffOpponent` in `src/engine/scenario.ts`, a stand-in: the packs hold no 2. Bundesliga), and meets the survival brief by winning it. The real 16th's play-off result is still not in the packs, so the real club's line still says it finished in the play-off place.
- **QA round 2's open items** ([QA.md](QA.md), round 2, R7-O1 to R7-O6): an arena shoot-out reads the opponent's reserve keeper as a 50/50 taker and a goalkeeping-30 keeper (`arena.ts`, bundled; needs a bump), an arena AI side stripped of the user's humans can field 9 or 10 (E6-2; needs a bump), a Dynasty keeps every summer's aged custom players (about 2,000 compressed characters a season), the opposition report leaves a reserve keeper out of its XI, an engine 5 Run-in keeps its unreconciled table, and the feel sweep's AI fixtures skip `aiFixtureInputs`.
- **QA round 3's open items** ([QA.md](QA.md), round 3, R8-O1 to R8-O4): the Gauntlet's alternates repeat a club already on the ladder (`gauntlet.ts`, bundled; an engine bump; the store now refuses a lifeline that would bring the same side back), `customPlayers` still grows about 2,000 compressed characters a Dynasty summer (R7-O3), two devices on different builds compare different sync keys until both update, and the bracket's round words and the match preview box are still English in every language.
- **A simmed week replays an event's draws** (r8 audit, not fixed: it moves seeded outcomes, so it needs an `ENGINE_VERSION` bump): `playMatchweekSteps` (`week.ts`, bundled) builds the week's `rng` from `game.rngState` before the assistant answers a pending event with `applyEventChoice`, which draws from the same state and writes `game.rngState`; the week's first fixtures then reuse the event's draws and line 425 overwrites its write. Only Sim 5, Sim to end and Sim to the next big match (autoManage) on about four weeks in ten. The fix moves `const rng = Rng.fromState(game.rngState)` to after the event block and `autoPick`; it changes `pins.test.ts`'s assistant season, `noMove.test.ts` and `marketPin.test.ts`'s January window, so it goes with the next engine bump.
- **Bundled cleanups waiting for the next verifier redeploy** (r8 audit, reported, not committed: each renames the edge bundle): dead `cupName` (`quickCups.ts`) and the unreachable `else q.continentalCup = run` after the continental path's return; dead `isBrief` (`runin.ts`), `continentalGroups` and the `GroupMatch` re-export (`week.ts`), `quickTierLabel` (`superLeague.ts`); `setup.ts`'s no-op `void playerLookup`, its `ordinal` (the engine's `ordinal` does the same) and `setupChips` (tests only, prints emoji flags); `modes.ts`'s `style === 'share' ? 'Quick Draft' : 'Quick Draft'`; `talks.ts`'s `surnameOf` beside `slotSheet.ts`'s `surname` (they differ on "Van Persie"); `story.ts`'s `realSeasonRow`, copied into `Summary.tsx`; `simulateAiCupTies` building `squadHumans` once per tie (`week.ts`); `runQuickSeason`'s doc still says "no injuries" (a bench season has them).

**Presentation, performance and accessibility**
- **A reload replays the reels** (`claude/polish-saves`): a reload during the draft's spin or the manager spin shows the reels again; the outcome is decided and saved before the reel turns, so it is presentation only. A reload during a live match resumes per tab only (another tab or device replays the segment).
- Performance (docs/PERFORMANCE.md, "Next" and "Round 6"): on a 4x-throttled phone the live match keeps the main thread about 90 % busy (the pitch's SVG repaint; the 30 fps cap takes about 5 % off); the week after Kick off still plays in one task (220 to 330 ms at 4x; splitting it means restructuring `week.ts`'s matchweek, with a seeded no-move test); the match screen's first render is about 300 ms; closing the January window and a bench change replay the quick season on the page (about 0.9 s); Home's first render is the start's longest task (about 375 ms at 4x); the in-memory game grows about 0.7 MB a live matchweek (the save is compacted, the memory is not). O-10's precache of every pack (about 10 MB before compression) is the owner's call.
- **Accessibility, left after the fourth pass** ([ACCESSIBILITY.md](ACCESSIBILITY.md) section 4): iOS text size waits for the wrapper to call `applyTextScale` with `@capacitor/text-zoom@8.0.1`; most component CSS is in px, so Dynamic Type grows the token text only; the match day's phone tool buttons, selects and tabs stay 31 to 32 px (the formation shirts' overlap is fixed in round 4); the Upgrader dial, the online screens signed in and a room in play are not audited, and nothing has been checked with VoiceOver on a real device. Two targets the spacing exception covers: the setup "i" clipped 3 px by its scrolling step at 1280 px, the depth chart's 23 px chips on a phone.
- Small UI leftovers: from the bug audit of 2bfa023, M5 (the game-by-game row tints), SD1 to SD3 and the career modal's unlabelled column, M11 (headings with an info button a few px over their box), M15 (wide empty halves in Stats and Club), M16 (the head-to-head reel replays after a reload); a few red club kits get badge ink at 4.4:1 (`inkOn`); some game-logic lines (story, setup chips) still carry emoji inside text; `--pitch-bad` is 1.9:1 on the grass; the arena hub keeps two inline px headings (`Arena.tsx`); at 1100x700 the pre-season coach mark scrolls inside the youth intake's row.

- Localisation (`claude/r7-i18n`): the words built outside `src/ui` are still English in every language: the engine's and the game logic's labels and lines (tier names, rule chips, news and inbox bodies, briefing and stop lines, verdicts and season stories, power-up, twist-mode and case texts, formation blurbs, role names, the tactics help texts, the market's and scouting's sentences, the run-in's and arena's lines, `authCopy.ts` and the online layer's messages), the match commentary (`src/ui/match/commentary*.ts`), the non-React match-day modules (`controls.ts`, `model.ts`, `widgetIds.ts`, `subBoard.ts`), and `moraleWord` in `format.ts`. Several of those files are bundled into the edge verifier, so moving their words into the UI is a release-time job (as `modeText.ts` and `trophyText.ts` mirror theirs, held equal by `i18n.test.ts`). The share text and share card stay English on purpose. `I18N_REPORT=1 npx vitest run src/ui/__tests__/i18nScreens.test.ts` lists what each screen still shows in English.

## 8. Roadmap at a glance

Tracked as GitHub issues, labelled by area (`area:engine`, `area:game`, `area:ui`, `area:data`, `area:online`, `area:ops`) and size (`size:S/M/L`); work being built carries `status:in-progress`: https://github.com/Minhhh13/Touchline/issues. Issues #3 to #12 and #19 describe work that has shipped and can be closed.

1. **Engine 6** (`release-e6`): release just before 00:00 UTC on 2026-10-28 with polish batch 2 and round 6, deploy `touchline-e6`, delete `touchline-e4` on or after 2026-11-03 and `touchline-e5` on or after 2026-11-04 (with their `public/edge/` bundles).
2. **Round 7** (`claude/r7-*`, about fifteen sessions): finishing touches after engine 6, released together after it.
3. **The iOS release** (issue #68, `ios-native`, PR #72): once the branch lands, the owner builds, signs and runs the app on a Mac and submits it to App Store Connect with the screenshots, following `docs/IOS_RELEASE.md` (written on that branch).
4. **The engine 7 release** (`batch4-e7`): engine 7, the Dynasty depth, error reports, the casual boards (the Daily Arena, the Weekly Challenge, the Run-in of the week) and the arena and Run-in cloud slots; the owner applies the three migrations first ([ONLINE.md](ONLINE.md) section 7, "Release engine 7").
5. **Supabase before a public launch**: switch on leaked-password protection (the one security-advisor warning), add Google sign-in (the owner's OAuth client) and Apple sign-in, and upgrade the same project to a paid plan.
6. Later: the next ranked items in [BACKLOG.md](BACKLOG.md), the data rebuild that strips the ",NN" names, classic seasons for the other leagues and in online head-to-head rooms, accessibility (#13), localisation (#15), a Playwright suite in the repo (#17), the next EA FC edition's data (#18).

## 9. Keeping this document current

This page is only useful while it is true. Every change, by either of us or by either of our AI agents, updates it in the same pull request: the in-progress table when work starts, moves or finishes; section 2 when a feature ships; section 6 when hosting or the backend changes; section 7 when an issue is found or fixed; and one line in the change log below. Agents find the same rule at the top of [CLAUDE.md](../CLAUDE.md) and in [AGENTS.md](../AGENTS.md). Add your line at the bottom of the log and never edit someone else's.

- 2026-10-26 · owner's agent · `online-rooms` (local worktree from `origin/main` fb4700d, not pushed): online Phase 2, head-to-head rooms. Two managers on two devices draft from one wheel exactly as the hot-seat does (`h2hRules` shared with the store; the engine's `h2hSpin` split into its draws and `h2hLand`, same draws, pins untouched, ENGINE_VERSION `'4'`), then the server plays `runH2HSeason`. Guests need no account: the Edge Function takes the publishable key for the `room.*` ops only (`withSupabase({ auth: ['user', 'publishable'] })`, so it must be redeployed with Verify JWT off), seats are random tokens stored only as SHA-256, guests are metered by a hash of their address. The server replays the room from its seed on every pick and keeps the seed secret until the season is played; clients rebuild the draft from the public log (`draftFromEvents`) and poll `public.h2h_peek` (2 to 15 s; Realtime rejected, reasons in [ONLINE.md](ONLINE.md) section 5e). Rooms close after 30 minutes unjoined, time out after 15 idle minutes, are purged 24 hours after they end (pg_cron `touchline-h2h-rooms`); Privacy page and `retention.test.ts` updated. New migration `20261026120000_h2h_rooms.sql` (not applied), SQL suite `phase2_rooms.sql`, allowlist adds `h2h_peek`; `check:verifier` now plays a guests' room through the bundle and compares it with the hot-seat game. UI: Head-to-head's This device / Play online switch (online builds only), make or join a room, the waiting room with code and link, the turn-by-turn draft, the result (the hot-seat result screen, now `H2HResultView`).

- 2026-10-26 · owner's agent · `engine-e5` TL-01 (P1): no automatic change brings a keeper-only man on outfield. Cause: `replacementFor` in `src/engine/match.ts` scored a keeper at outfield as `overall × 0.35` and took him when he was the only man left on the bench (Season, Dynasty, quick seasons and the arena share it; the assistant's own stop calls in `assistant.ts` already refused keepers). Fix: `eligibleReplacement` (an outfielder may still go in goal for an injured keeper, the emergency case) and the same guard in the quick season's injury cover (`fieldXI`, `quickBench.ts`). Before: Gauntlet seed 3NVT-9VDF, drafted by the assistant, brought Ederson on for Mbabu (RB, 61') and De Gea for Fletcher (ST, 88'); 25 of 80 assistant arena runs and 5 of 1,140 Season matches had one. After: none (`keeperSubs.test.ts`, engine and game).
- 2026-10-26 · owner's agent · `engine-e5` TL-05 (P2): fixtures alternate home and away. Cause: `generateFixtures` (`src/engine/season.ts`) alternated venues along each side of the circle only, so a club kept one venue for a whole stint and the shuffled rounds and mirrored second half gave blocks of a half-season; head-to-head seed QEWA-3V9H had Manager 2 away in MW1-19 and home in MW20-38. Fix: the clubs shuffled onto the canonical (de Werra) circle schedule, the second half mirrored with its first round moved to the end: at most two home or away running for every club, every size 2 to 24 (odd fields with a bye), no rematch in consecutive games; one draw instead of two shuffles. Shared by Season, Dynasty, Quick Draft, the Daily, the Weekly, the Run-in and head-to-head (`season.test.ts`, `fixtureVenues.test.ts`).
- 2026-10-26 · owner's agent · `engine-e5` TL-04 (P2): the assistant places a man where he plays best. Cause: `h2hAutoPick` (`src/engine/h2h.ts`) took the highest overall into `eligibleSlots(...)[0]`, the first slot in formation order (Grimes DM/CM to centre-back with both CM slots open); Quick Draft's `assistantChoice` charged every cover a flat 0.92 with formation order breaking ties. Fix: `bestSlotFor` in `draft.ts` (best `positionFactor`, then the position with the fewest open slots, then formation order), `assistantChoice` scoring rating × the real factor, `h2hAutoPick` and `suggestSlot` through it. Over 120 assistant head-to-head drafts: cover slots 57.9% to 0.8%, mean positional cost 7.11% to 0.08%; picks that took a cover while a natural slot was open 776 of 2,640 to 0 (`assistantFit.test.ts`).
- 2026-10-26 · owner's agent · `engine-e5` INV-01 (P2, confirmed): large drops at the first development update were a reset, not development. `applyDelta` (`development.ts`) and the dynasty summer's `ageOneYear` (`dynasty.ts`) stored `overallOf(attributes)`, and the imported overall sits 2.6 points above that on average (78,058 player-seasons; up to 46): run-in seed TX3U-SU25's digest (Bennett 72→63, Doucouré 81→77, Kane 89→84) is exactly each man's derived figure, as are Pavard 80→75 and Bellingham 86→81(+1) in the Dynasty.
